H71: WHAT CLUSTERING COSTS AT THE EDGE OF CAPACITY. Apple M4. ALL ARMS SATURATED.
Run 2026-11-19. Thresholds fixed before the run and committed as 6f6a5621034e049ca2f0c10a11289a2eb35805fc.
Verdicts recorded 2026-11-19 in docs/experiments.md and summarised below.

MACHINE : Mac16,13, 10 cpus
OS      : ProductVersion 27.2, BuildVersion 26B123
RUSTC   : rustc 1.92.0 (ded5c06cf 2025-12-08)
COMMIT  : 6f6a5621034e049ca2f0c10a11289a2eb35805fc
POWER   : AC at the start and at the end of the sweep
LAUNCHES: 30 of 30, 28m 16s
RATE    : 10,000,000 offered, 10,000,000 messages per epoch

WHY THIS FILE HAS NO CCDF LINES. The instrument does not emit a quantile
ladder for a saturated run, because the percentiles of a saturated run
describe the queue rather than the system. Every one of the 1,080 cells
saturated, so the file carries the 1,080 pl lines and nothing else. That
is H71 (f), which was registered before the run, and the run script
stopped rather than committing a file of excluded rows or lowering the
rate until something passed.

PRE-FLIGHT, IMMEDIATELY BEFORE THE SWEEP, AND IT CONTRADICTS IT.
Same binary, same 10,000,000 rate, 2,000,000 messages per epoch:
  load=rate_10000000  saturated=0  offered_mps=10.000  achieved_mps=9.848
  load=hawkes_0.0     saturated=0  offered_mps=10.001  achieved_mps=10.001
  load=hawkes_0.8     saturated=1  offered_mps=9.985   achieved_mps=8.488
That is the asymmetry H71 (a) predicted. At five times the messages per
epoch the periodic arm goes under as well, from 1.5 percentage points
above the 97 percent gate. The gate was crossed by DURATION, not by rate.
It is one cell per arm and it is POST HOC with respect to this
registration. E3, in results/2026-11-19_m4_envelope.txt, measures it.

LAUNCH WALL TIMES. 26 of 30 launches took 46 or 47 seconds. Four took
110s, 158s, 63s and 100s, in a 28-minute continuous sweep on a fanless
chassis. Recorded, not explained.
